   getScore_fire_tree1(R, T, TreesToUse, Total1),
   getScore_fire_tree2(R, T, TreesToUse, Total2),
   getScore_fire_tree3(R, T, TreesToUse, Total3),
   getScore_fire_tree4(R, T, TreesToUse, Total4),
   getScore_fire_tree5(R, T, TreesToUse, Total5),
   Total is LogPrior + Total1 + Total2 + Total3 + Total4 + Total5,
   !.
